- **hire-assess-fit**: Run a culture-add fit assessment for a candidate (NOT culture-fit). Names what the team currently lacks, names what this candidate adds, runs gap-bridge analysis. ([Starlight-Intelligence/Starlight-Intelligence-System](https://github.com/Starlight-Intelligence/Starlight-Intelligence-System/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/hire-assess-fit.md) · ★11 · 2 copies)
- **hire-calibrate**: Run the pre-loop calibration session — 60 minutes with ≥3 raters, review the rubric, score 2 anchor candidates the team has previously seen, surface inter-rater drift, agree on hire-bar examples. The calibration session itself is the deliverable. Without it, the loop runs on uncalibrated rulers. Per Project Oxygen — cross-rater alignment beats rater quality. Not legal advice. ([Starlight-Intelligence/Starlight-Intelligence-System](https://github.com/Starlight-Intelligence/Starlight-Intelligence-System/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/hire-calibrate.md) · ★11 · 2 copies)

- **hire-debrief**: Run the post-loop decision debrief — facilitator script for the 45-60 minute session. Structured scores submitted BEFORE discussion (kills loud-voice halo). Tie-breaks default to hire-no. Not legal advice.
